State violence and shootings of Kolbars have led to protests in the past.

[EVENT: IRN25301]
Date: 2023-11-08
Country: Iran | Location: Tehran - District 1
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Actor2: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: BBC News; Iran International [International]

DESCRIPTION:
On 8 November 2023, a warning about the observance of hijab by a religious member of 'enjoining good and forbidding wrong' caused a verbal clash, and a security guard pushed a female student at Velayat University Complex, Islamic Azad University Central Tehran Branch in Tehran - District 1 (Tehran). The student fell and was then taken to the hospital. After the Mahsa movement, the 'hijab' became a sociopolitical matter, and the religious leaders whom the government and the police support daily tried to prevent anti-veil activities by Iranian women.

---

[EVENT: IRN25653]
Date: 2023-11-23
Country: Iran | Location: Tehran - District 12
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Actor2: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Human Rights Activists in Iran (HRA) [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 23 November 2023, a warning about the observance of hijab by two religious members of the 'enjoining good and forbidding wrong' group - Iran's morality police - led to a violent clash at Lorzadeh Street in Tehran - District 12 (Tehran). According to the report, the members of the morality police stopped two women riding a motorcycle for failing to observe the mandatory hijab laws, causing a verbal argument between the two sides. A bystander armed with a cold weapon intervened and injured one of the morality police members. Another bystander who attempted to intervene was also injured by the attacker. One person was arrested by the police in relation to the incident, while a manhunt order was issued for a second unidentified person. There were no fatalities. After the Mahsa movement, the 'hijab' became a sociopolitical matter, and the religious leaders whom the government and the police support daily tried to prevent anti-veil activities by Iranian women.

---

[EVENT: IRN25921]

Country: Iran | Location: Rasak
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Army of Justice [Rebel group]
Actor2: Police Forces of Iran (1989-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 13
Sources: Baloch Campaign; BBC News; Eteraze Bazar; FARS News Agency (Iran); Haalvash; Human Rights Activists in Iran (HRA); Iran International; Iranian Student News Agency; Tasnim News Agency; Young Journalists Club [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 15 December 2023, gunmen of the Army of Justice clashed with Iranian police officers after they fired at a police station in Rasak city (Sistan and Baluchestan). The clash lasted for about 5 hours. As a result, at least 11 police officers and 2 members of the Army of Justice were killed, and 7 police officers were wounded. A member of the Army of Justice was injured and arrested during the clash. The Army of Justice claimed that 50 police officers were killed during the clash, but this was not corroborated independently.
